    window.executeOnScriptsLoaded(function () {
        window.addEvent('domready', function () {
            $('socialLeftFacebook').addEvent('mouseover', function () {
                if (window.FB == undefined) {
                    (function (d, s, id) {
                        var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];
                        if (d.getElementById(id)) return;
                        js = d.createElement(s);
                        js.id = id;
                        js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/en_US/sdk.js#xfbml=1&version=v3.2";
                        fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fjs);
                    }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));
                }
            });

            $$('.menuTop').addEvent('mouseover', function () {

                    }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));

                if (!window.gapi) {
                    new Asset.javascript('https://apis.google.com/js/platform.js')
                }
            });

            $('socialLeftFacebook').addEvent('mouseenter', function () {
                if (!$('socialWrapperFacebook').retrieve('facebook')) {
                    $('socialWrapperFacebook').set('html', '<div id="facebookWrapperSpinner" class="spinner" style="transform:scale(0.7);margin:50px auto;margin-top:70px;position:relative;width:10px;height:10px;background:none;"><div class="spinner-img"></div></div><div class="fb-page" id="fb-page-header" data-href="https://www.facebook.com/rankingCoach.en" data-width="380" data-height="130" data-hide-cover="false" data-show-facepile="false" data-show-posts="false"></div>');
                    $('socialWrapperFacebook').store('facebook', true);
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        FB.XFBML.parse(document.getElementById('socialWrapperFacebook'), function () {
                            $('facebookWrapperSpinner').destroy();
                        });
                    }, 500);
                }
            });



            $('social_right_twitter').addEvent('mouseover', function () {
                !function (d, s, id) {
                    var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0], p = /^http:/.test(d.location) ? 'http' : 'https';
                    if (!d.getElementById(id)) {
                        js = d.createElement(s);

                    }
                }(document, 'script', 'twitter-wjs');
            });
        })

<script type="text/javascript">
	window.executeOnScriptsLoaded(function () {
		new UISlideShow();
		var animationInProgress = false;
		var lastAnimation = -1;
		var lastPercent = null;

    function homeDigitalPresenceReveal() {
			if (animationInProgress)
				return;
			var container = $$('.homeDigitialPresenceDemo');
			if (!container)
				return;
			var container_y = container[0].getPosition().y;
			var scroll_y = window.getScroll().y;
			var container_height = container[0].getHeight();
			container_y -= $('menuTopWrapper').getHeight();
			// we need to see how much % of the image is "visible", we add 75% of image container beccause we need the reveal to start later
			var percent = (scroll_y - container_y + (container_height * 0.9)) * 100 / (container_height);
			if (percent < 0 || percent >= 200)
				return;
			//console.log(lastAnimation, percent, lastPercent);
			var reveal = -1;
			if (percent > lastPercent && percent >= 45)
				reveal = 1;
			else if (percent < lastPercent)
				reveal = 0;
			lastPercent = percent;

			animationInProgress = true;
			var reveal_container = $$('.homeDigitialPresenceDemo .reveal_animation');
			//  || percent >= 45
			if (reveal == 1) {
				if (lastAnimation === 1) {
					animationInProgress = false;
					return;
				}
				reveal_container.removeClass('final').removeClass('reverse');
				(function () {
					reveal_container.addClass('final');
				}).delay(10);
				lastAnimation = 1;
			}
			// || percent <= 30
			if (reveal == 0) {
				if (lastAnimation === 0 || lastAnimation === -1) {
					animationInProgress = false;
					return;
				}
				reveal_container.removeClass('final').removeClass('reverse');
				container.getElement('.optimised').removeClass('final');
				(function () {
					reveal_container.addClass('final reverse');
				}).delay(10);
				lastAnimation = 0;
			}
			(function () {
				animationInProgress = false;
				if (reveal == 1)
					container.getElement('.optimised').addClass('final');
			}).delay(1500);
		}

		new UICallwhenvisible($$('.homeDigitialPresenceDemo'), {
			events: {
				visible: function (element) {
					//return;
					element.addClass('final');
					homeDigitalPresenceReveal();
					window.addEvent('scroll', function () {
						window.requestAnimationFrame(homeDigitalPresenceReveal)
					});
				}
			}
		});
		new UICallwhenvisible($$('.ui_call_when_visible'), {
			events: {
				visible: function (element) {
					//return;
					element.addClass('final');
				}
			}
		});
		new UICallwhenvisible($$('.homeDevicesContainer'), {
			events: {
				visible: function (element) {
					element.addClass('final');
					(function () {
						showMacVideo();
					}).delay(1600);
				}
			}
		});

    new UIOverlayBox($('cookies-not-enabled-overlay'), {
      content: $('cookies-not-enabled-warning'),
      overlayStyles: {
        contentClass: 'no-padding',
        showTitleBarOutside: true,
        scrollable: false,
        size: {
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth: '820px'
        },
        overlay_content: {
          classes: 'no-padding'
        }
      },
      cloneContent: true,
      events: {
        open: function () {
          (function () {
            UIOverlayBox.close();
          }).delay(100000);
        }
      }
    });

    if(!navigator.cookieEnabled){
      $('cookies-not-enabled-overlay').overlaybox.build();
    }
	});
</script>
